## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter，你的个人 AI 伙伴。比如你可以让我去修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your partner on this machine. For example, I can track down a failing test in one of your repos and tell you when it's fixed.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 바로 할 수 있는 일 하나를 들면, 어떤 저장소에서 깨진 테스트를 찾아 고쳐 드릴 수 있어요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.

### direct-es

```

```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？

```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase when the branch is yours alone and not pushed yet, to tidy it onto the latest base. Merge when others have pulled the branch or the history must stay as it happened, because rebasing rewrites commits and breaks anyone building on them.
```

--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在 `bun test` 后面就行：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts
```

如果只想跑某个测试用例，可以再加 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test tests/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例的名字"
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is an append-only file where a database records each change before applying it to the main data files. After a crash, it replays that log to recover committed changes and discard incomplete ones, so writes stay durable and fast.
```

(function () {
  var cnt = document.getElementById('cnt');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"]/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;' }[c];
    });
  }

  function render(d) {
    var all = (d && d.trains) || [];
    var limit = (d && d.limit) || 600;
    var ok = all.filter(function (t) { return t.price <= limit; })
                .sort(function (a, b) { return a.price - b.price; });

    if (!all.length) {
      cnt.innerHTML = '<span class="k-none">还没查</span>';
      sub.textContent = '每天早上更新';
      list.innerHTML = '';
      empty.hidden = false;
      if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary('还没查');
      return;
    }

    empty.hidden = true;
    cnt.textContent = ok.length + ' 班';
    sub.textContent = ok.length
      ? '最低 ' + ok[0].price + ' 元 · 查于 ' + (d.checked || '')
      : '没有 ' + limit + ' 元以下的班次 · 查于 ' + (d.checked || '');

    list.innerHTML = all.map(function (t) {
      var good = t.price <= limit;
      var seats = t.seats == null ? '余票未知' : '二等座余 ' + t.seats + ' 张';
      return '<div class="k-row">' +
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + esc(t.no) + (good ? '' : ' · 超过 ' + limit) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(seats) + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value" style="color:var(' + (good ? '--d-good' : '--d-muted') + ')">' + esc(t.price) + ' 元</div>' +
        '</div>';
    }).join('');

    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(ok.length + ' 班 600 元以下');
  }

  function load(v) {
    if (v && typeof v.then === 'function') v.then(render);
    else render(v);
  }

  if (window.ducter) {
    load(ducter.get('board'));
    ducter.onChange(function () { load(ducter.get('board')); });
  }
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"board":{"day":"周五","limit":600,"checked":"2026-11-26","trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}}
  [Ducter passed the user's answer to task fares] [Ducter] The person now keeps this work's rounds in their small app "周五京沪高铁票价" (app a2d44985f). Each round, write what stands now into it with app_data (app a2d44985f, set {key: value}; null removes a key); the app reads the keys board. Write whole values (a key's value is replaced), once a round. Then don't report_to_main for a round that only updated it: report only when something needs the pers
  [also used tools] app

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">Latest run</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="w">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="p">writes/s, p99 latency</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">Nightly runs</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
</div>

  body{background:var(--d-bg);color:var(--d-ink);margin:0}
  .k-row-sub{color:var(--d-muted)}

  .k-row-value.bad{color:var(--d-bad)}
</style>
<script>
  function render(runs) {
    runs = Array.isArray(runs) ? runs : [];
    var list = document.getElementById('list');
    var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
    list.innerHTML = '';
    empty.hidden = runs.length > 0;
    if (runs.length) {
      var last = runs[runs.length - 1];
      document.getElementById('w').textContent = last.writes + ' writes/s';
      document.getElementById('p').textContent = 'p99 ' + last.p99 + ' · compaction stalls ' + last.stalls + ' · ' + last.night;
    }
    runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      row.innerHTML =
        '<div class="k-row-main">' + r.night + ' · ' + r.date + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">p99 ' + r.p99 + ' · stalls ' + r.stalls + '</div>' +
        '<div class="k-row-value">' + r.writes + '/s</div>';
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
  }
  Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(render);
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(render);
  });

.k-sum-value{font-size:40px;font-weight:700;color:var(--d-ink);margin:6px 0}
.k-sum-sub{font-size:14px;color:var(--d-soft)}
.k-h{font-size:16px;margin:8px 4px}
</style>
<script>
function draw(){
const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
const list=document.getElementById("list");
list.innerHTML="";
let sum=0;
for(const x of xs){
sum+=Number(x.yuan)||0;
const row=document.createElement("div");
row.className="k-row";
const main=document.createElement("div");
main.className="k-row-main";
main.textContent=x.what;
const sub=document.createElement("div");
sub.className="k-row-sub";
sub.textContent=x.day;
main.appendChild(sub);
const val=document.createElement("div");
val.className="k-row-value";
val.textContent="¥"+x.yuan;
row.appendChild(main);
row.appendChild(val);
list.appendChild(row);
}
document.getElementById("total").textContent="¥"+sum;
document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length+" 笔";
if(!xs.length){const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="还没记";list.appendChild(e);}
ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 ¥"+sum);
}
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

  [partner message] 做好了，11/20 的高铁和两晚酒店已经从你的预订里填进去了。以后记一笔就自动加总。

    <div class="k-sum-value" id="total"></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub"></div>
  </div>

  <h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
    <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没记一笔</div>
  </div>

  <details class="k-add">
    <summary>记一笔</summary>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="what">花在哪</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="打车、午饭…"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="day">日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="11/21"></div>
    <div class="k-field"><label for="amt">金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
    <button class="k-btn primary" id="add">记上</button>
  </details>
</main>

<style>
  .k-row { cursor: pointer; }
  .k-add summary { cursor: pointer; }
  .k-add .k-field { margin-top: 10px; }
  .k-add .k-btn { margin-top: 12px; width: 100%; }
</style>

<script>
  var items = [];

  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { '&': '&amp;', '<': '&lt;', '>': '&gt;', '"': '&quot;', "'": '&#39;' }[c];
    }) …    ducter.set('expenses', items);
    render();
  }

  document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var what = document.getElementById('what').value.trim();
    var day = document.getElementById('day').value.trim();
    var amount = parseFloat(document.getElementById('amt').value);
    if (!what || !(amount > 0)) return;
    items.push({ id: 'e' + Date.now(), what: what, day: day, amount: amount, from: '' });
    document.getElementById('what').value = '';
    document.getElementById('amt').value = '';
    document.getElementById('day').value = '';
    document.querySelector('.k-add').open = false;
    save();
  });

  document.getElementById('list').addEventListener('click', async function (e) {
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (!row) return;
    var id = row.getAttribute('data-id');
    var item = items.find(function (it) { return it.id === id; });
    if (!item) return;
    var answer = await ducter.ask('删掉「' + item.what + '」这笔？');
    if (answer === 'go' || answer === true || answer === 'yes') {
      items = items.filter(function (it) { return it.id !== id; });
      save();
    }
  });

  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"id":"e1","what":"高铁 G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00","day":"11/20","amount":662,"from":"你的预订"},{"id":"e2","what":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚","day":"11/20–21","amount":1036,"from":"你的预订"}]}

    <button class … on (e) {
    var r = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (r) openEdit(r.dataset.id);
  });

  $('aAdd').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var amt = parseAmt($('aAmt').value);
    if (amt === null) { $('aAmt').focus(); return; }
    items.push({ id: uid(), amount: amt, cat: addCat, note: $('aNote').value.trim(), date: today() });
    $('aAmt').value = '';
    $('aNote').value = '';
    save();
  });

  $('eSave').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var amt = parseAmt($('eAmt').value);
    if (amt === null) { $('eAmt').focus(); return; }
    items = items.map(function (x) {
      return x.id === editId ? { id: x.id, amount: amt, cat: editCat, note: $('eNote').value.trim(), date: x.date } : x;
    });
    closeEdit();
    save();
  });

  $('eDel').addEventListener('click', function () {
    items = items.filter(function (x) { return x.id !== editId; });
    closeEdit();
    save();
  });

  $('eCancel').addEventListener('click', closeEdit);

  function load() {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('spends')).then(function (v) {
      items = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
      render();
    });
  }

  drawChips($('aCats'), addCat);
  ducter.onChange(load);
  load();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {}

    landed = landed.indexOf(id) >= 0
      ? landed.filter(function (x) { return x !== id; })
      : landed.concat([id]);
    save();
    render();
  }

  function init(v) {
    landed = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
    render();
  }

  if (window.ducter && ducter.get) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(init, function () { init([]); });
    if (ducter.onChange) ducter.onChange(function () {
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get('landed')).then(function (v) {
        landed = Array.isArray(v) ? v : [];
        render();
      });
    });
  } else {
    init([]);
  }

### app-renew-zh

```
(earlier) user: 帮我订明天去北京的高铁，再订两晚酒店，离 agibar 近一点
(earlier) partner: 订好了：11/20 G1 07:00 上海虹桥到北京南，¥662；亚朵酒店（望京）11/20 和 11/21 两晚，¥1,036。
--- step 1: user types in the sheet of their app (the first one listed in [apps]), about it: 把「出差花销」换成新样子，能填的先帮我填好
  [partner message] 做好了，「出差花销」已经换成新样子，高铁和两晚酒店的两笔也填进去了，合计 ¥1,698。
  [an app card under the message: changed "出差花销", with Open] the app's file now (v2, 2429 characters): <main class="k-app"><div class="k-sum"><div class="k-sum-label">出差花销合计</div><div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div><div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">还没记</div></div><h2 class="k-h">花销明细</h2><div class="k-card"><div class="k-list" id="list"></div><div class="k-empty" id="empty">还没记。在下面「记一笔」里加第一笔。</div></div><details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary><div class="k-card"><div class="k-field"><label>出差名称</label><input class="k-input" id="trip" value="北京出差 11/20–21"></div><div class="k-chips" id="cats"><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="true" data-c="交通">交通</button><button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="false" data-c="住宿">住宿</button></div><div class="k-field"><label>金额 (¥)</label><input class="k-input k-num" id="amt" inputmode="decimal"></div><button class="k-btn primary" id="save">保存</button></div></details></main><script>
let cat="交通";
const fmt=n=>"¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN");
document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(b=>b.onclick=()=>{cat=b.dataset.c;document.querySelectorAll("#cats .k-chip").forEach(x=>x.setAttribute("aria-pressed",String(x===b)))});
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.textContent="";
  let total=0;
  xs.forEach(x=>{
    total+=Number(x.amt)||0;
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";
    const t=document.createElement("div");t.textContent=x.trip||"";
    const s=document.createElement("div");s.className="k-row-sub";s.textContent=[x.cat,x.note].filter(Boolean).join(" · ");
    main.append(t,s);
    const v=document.createElement("div");v.className="k-row-value";v.textContent=fmt(x.amt);
    row.append(main,v);list.append(row);
  });
  document.getElementById("empty").style.display=xs.length?"none":"block";
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=fmt(total);
  document.getElementById("sub").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  ducter.summary(xs.length?xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total):"还没记");
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=()=>{
  const amt=parseFloat(document.getElementById("amt").value);
  if(!amt||isNaN(amt))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("items")||[];
  xs.push({trip:document.getElementById("trip").value.trim()||"出差",cat:cat,amt:amt});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  document.getElementById("amt").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","cat":"交通","amt":662,"note":"G1 上海虹桥→北京南 07:00"},{"trip":"北京出差 11/20–21","cat":"住宿","amt":1036,"note":"亚朵酒店（望京）两晚"}]}

--- step 1: user says: 帮我启动一个 codex 的 loop agent，名字就叫 Nowledge Mem PR babysitter（之类的名字），在 Nowledge Bridge 里你起好名字，我可以让我自己的 agent 在 PR 到一定阶段之后把它们丢给你，然后你就可以帮忙盯着，做需要的修改修复 ci failure，或者如果涉及到别的人的 review，帮忙做修复，如果你能自决修复的，就不断修复 push 然后看着，直到工作可以被合并，再 bridge 回信表示工作做完了

我们 bridge 不知道是否有唤醒能力，如果没有，暂时需要你自己去 loop 唤醒自己读取 bridge 信箱
  [partner message] 好，Nowledge Mem PR babysitter 已经起好了。你的 agent 在 Nowledge Bridge 里把 PR 交给它，它就会接手，修 CI、修 review 意见，能自己定的就直接 push，做完了再回信给你的 agent。它是按来信唤醒的，不用我自己一直轮询信箱。

不过 Codex 这一项我指定不了，它现在跑在我这边的默认 agent 上。你要的话，得在设置里给它换成 Codex。
  [also used tools] keep_make, without_asking, keep_change
--- what Ducter sent through Bridge (other agents see this, the user doesn't):
```

### wp-at-task-followup-zh

```
(earlier) user: 修一下 foo 登录页的重定向循环
(earlier) partner ack: 好，我来修 foo 登录页的重定向循环。  → task "修复 foo 登录页的重定向循环" done; its report: 修好了：登录后的 next 参数没校验，回跳到 /login 自己。加了白名单和 3 个测试，Chrome 下全过。
(earlier) user: 再给 foo 的写入路径做个压测
(earlier) partner ack: 好，开始压测 foo 的写入路径。  → task "压测 foo 的写入路径" working
(earlier) user: 压测跑到哪了
(earlier) partner: 还在跑，第二轮了，预计十分钟出结果。
--- step 1: user says: @修复 foo 登录页的重定向循环 Safari 下也验证一遍，补个测试 (picked in the composer: @ the task "login")
  [partner ack line] 好，我让修复登录页的那个任务在 Safari 下再验证一遍，并补一个测试，做完告诉你。
  [sent to task login] 用户原话：「@修复 foo 登录页的重定向循环 Safari 下也验证一遍，补个测试」

请在这个任务的分支（ducter/foo）上完成：
1. 在 Safari 下验证登录页的重定向循环已修复（登录后 next 参数被校验、不会回跳到 /login），把验证步骤和结果写进报告。
2. 补一个测试，覆盖 Safari 场景下的回跳行为，并确认它在修复前会失败、修复后通过。
3. 提交到本任务的分支即可。

不要合并到基础分支，不要推送，不要开 PR。如果 Safari 下没法跑，直接说明原因，不要用 Chrome 结果代替。
  [retitled task login] 修复 foo 登录页的重定向循环，并在 Safari 下验证、补测试
```
